The selected variables are organized by deployment-time availability, separating past-known sensor states from future-known calendar, schedule, and meteorological exogenous variables. Across three real-world buildings, TopoBrick outperforms strong zero-shot foundation-model baselines and remains competitive with fully trained building-specific models. Ablations show that topology-aware sampling is more reliable than random, ontology-only, or fixed-hop selection, especially for physically coupled HVAC and weather-driven sensing variables.

</details>


### [36] [A Definition and Roadmap for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06401)
*Xinyuan Chen,Haoyu Guo,Shi Guo,Bingqi Jiang,Chunhua Shen,Xing Shen,Tianfan Xue,Yufei Xue,Mulin Yu,Weinan Zhang,Bin Zhao,Bowen Zhou,Ming Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了世界模型的科学定义、关键技术方面和发展路线图，旨在解决AI各子领域对世界模型概念认识不统一的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI各子领域（如基于模型的强化学习、视频生成、具身机器人和物理AI）都在构建称为"世界模型"的系统，但对世界模型的基本定义、预测目标和构建方法缺乏共识，亟需一个统一的科学框架。

Method: 本文采用视角综述（perspective article）的方法，通过提供世界模型的科学定义、讨论关键技术方面，并制定一个分阶段的发展路线图来系统性地阐述世界模型。

Result: 给出了世界模型的科学定义，明确了其预测目标，并提出了构建有效世界模型的分阶段路线图。

Conclusion: 世界模型是学习环境结构和动态的内部模拟器，其发展需要遵循分阶段路线图，以推动从强化学习到物理AI等各领域的发展。

Abstract: World models -- internal simulators that learn the structure and dynamics of an environment -- have become one of the most actively debated concepts in AI. From model-based reinforcement learning and video generation to embodied robotics and ultimately, physical AI, researchers across AI subfields are building systems that they call "world models", yet there is no consensus on what a world model fundamentally is, what it should predict, or how it should be built. This perspective article provides a scientific definition of world models, discussions of their key technical aspects, and a staged roadmap for developing effective world models.

</details>


### [37] [ExplAIner: A Declarative Query Language for Explaining Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06407)
*Marcelo Arenas,Pablo Barceló,Diego Bustamante,Jose Caraball,María Alejandra Schild,Bernardo Subercaseaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对布尔模型提出了一种可解释性查询语言ExplAIner，克服了现有FOIL语言无法表达最优性解释查询及其在决策树上的高阶复杂度问题。ExplAIner能够统一表达反事实、对比、特征和距离等多种解释查询，且每个查询的评估问题在可多项式时间评估基本谓词的布尔模型类上属于布尔层次。优化片段Opt-FOIL可在FP^NP复杂度内计算最小解释，算法上只需常数或多项式次SAT求解器调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI社区提出了大量不同的解释查询和评分方法，但缺乏一种声明式查询语言来统一规范、组合和分析这些解释概念。现有FOIL语言存在两个根本局限：无法表达基于最优性的核心解释查询，且在决策树上的评估问题对多项式层次的每一层都是困难的。

Method: 在FOIL基础上扩展词汇并引入分层结构得到ExplAIner查询语言；定义Opt-FOIL作为面向严格偏序最小解释的优化片段；通过将评估问题归约到布尔层次和FP^NP复杂度类进行理论分析。

Result: ExplAIner可表达包括反事实、对比、特征和距离在内的广泛解释概念；每个ExplAIner查询在可多项式时间评估基本谓词的布尔模型类上属于布尔层次；Opt-FOIL的评估问题在相同可处理性假设下属于FP^NP。

Conclusion: 固定ExplAIner查询可用常数次SAT求解器调用评估，Opt-FOIL中指定的解释概念可用多项式次数调用计算，这对基于SAT求解器的形式化XAI具有重要实际意义。

Abstract: The XAI community has studied a wide range of queries and scores for explaining predictions of ML models. From a data management perspective, this proliferation of explanation notions calls for declarative query languages in which such notions can be specified, combined, and analyzed uniformly. In this paper, we develop such a framework for Boolean models. We first revisit FOIL, an interpretability query language for black-box models, and show that it has two fundamental limitations: it cannot express central optimality-based explanation queries, and its evaluation problem over decision trees is hard for every level of the polynomial hierarchy. We then introduce ExplAIner, a query language based on FOIL with an extended vocabulary and a layered structure. We show that ExplAIner can express a broad family of explanation notions, including abductive, contrastive, feature-based, and distance-based queries. We also prove that the evaluation problem for each query in ExplAIner belongs to the Boolean hierarchy over every class of Boolean models for which some basic predicates can be evaluated in polynomial time. In particular, that property holds for deterministic and decomposable Boolean circuits. Finally, we introduce Opt-FOIL, an optimization-oriented fragment of ExplAIner for computing explanations that are minimal with respect to strict partial orders, and prove that its evaluation problem is in $\mathrm{FP}^{\mathrm{NP}}$ under the same tractability assumptions. These complexity results have a direct algorithmic consequence: a fixed ExplAIner query can be evaluated with a fixed number of calls to a SAT solver, while a notion of explanation specified in Opt-FOIL can be computed with a polynomial number of such calls. This is particularly relevant in formal XAI, where SAT solvers have been successfully used to compute explanations for several classes of ML models.

</details>


### [38] [Finding H. pylori in the Fine Print: Evidence-Linked Multi-Agent Case Finding from Gastric Biopsy Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06435)
*Yufan Wang,Anit Kumar Sahu,Yan Fei Ng,Daniel Kang,Shayan Vassef,Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Koustuv Saha,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Chee Leong Cheng,Li Yan Khor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种基于多智能体系统的命名实体识别和证据链接工作流（nMAS），用于从病理报告中自动提取幽门螺杆菌感染及相关胃炎信息，在216项特征-病例判定中达到98.61%的准确率，并显著降低人工审查时间（从83.3小时降至1.4小时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幽门螺杆菌感染与慢性活动性胃炎、消化性溃疡及胃癌预防密切相关，但相关证据分散在异构的结构化和自由文本报告字段中，需要上下文断言和否定解释，导致关键词搜索受限、人工审查难以规模化。

Method: 回顾性试点评估：使用54份去标识化胃活检病理报告，采用字段名驱动的多智能体系统（nMAS），提取四个临床二元字段（胃活检、活检状态、H. pylori阳性、H. pylori相关胃炎），并以UMA风格的MiniMax M2.5作为比较基准。

Result: nMAS在216项特征-病例判定中正确分类213项，总体准确率98.61%，与对比模型在聚合和逐字段指标上表现相似。nMAS输出保持统一报告级别并附带支持性源句，具备工作流集成与可追溯性优势。

Conclusion: nMAS的主要贡献在于工作流集成和可追溯性，而非预测性能优越性；在模拟场景下可将人工审查时间从83.3小时降至1.4小时。未来需在更大规模多机构研究中验证证据跨度正确性、临床医师验证时间及泛化能力。

Abstract: Data from Singapore indicated that about 31% of the population had evidence of Helicobacter pylori infection. Persistent H. pylori infection is associated with chronic active gastritis and peptic ulcer disease, and its eradication is key to gastric cancer prevention. However, evidence supporting \textit{H. pylori} positivity and H. pylori-associated gastritis may be distributed across heterogeneous coded and free-text report fields and may require contextual interpretation of assertion and negation, limiting keyword search, and making manual review difficult to scale. We conducted a retrospective pilot evaluation of the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), a field-name-driven, evidence-linked extraction workflow, using 54 de-identified gastric biopsy pathology reports from a large healthcare system in Singapore. Four clinician-scoped binary fields were evaluated: gastric/stomach biopsy, biopsy status, H. pylori positivity, and H. pylori-associated gastritis. Across 216 feature-case decisions, nMAS correctly classified 213, corresponding to 98.61% overall accuracy. A separately implemented UMA-style MiniMax M2.5 comparator produced similar aggregate and per-field classification metrics. Although predictive performance was similar, nMAS maintained unified report-level outputs with supporting source sentences; the demonstrated contribution is therefore workflow integration and traceability rather than predictive superiority. Under an illustrative, unmeasured scenario, reviewing 1,000 reports at five minutes per manual review versus five seconds per evidence-linked verification would reduce review time from 83.3 to 1.4 staff-hours, corresponding to 81.9 staff-hours and about USD~6,100 in potential staff-time value. Larger multi-institutional studies should evaluate evidence-span correctness, clinician verification time, and generalizability.

</details>


### [39] [Danus: Orchestrating Mathematical Reasoning Agents with Fact-Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06447)
*Jihao Liu,Guoxiong Gao,Zeming Sun,Bin Wu,Shurui Liu,Jiedong Jiang,Haocheng Ju,Leheng Chen,Ronnie Cheng,Xiping Zhang,Bin Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Danus是一个基于事实图（fact graph）作为全局内存管理机制的数学推理编排系统，通过主智能体规划协调、多个工作智能体并行证明搜索、以及无状态验证器检查命题，实现了面向研究级数学问题的长程证明构建，在代数几何、奇点理论和组合学的六个研究级案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的数学推理智能体虽已能处理研究级问题甚至贡献于开放难题的解决，但在扩展和编排多个智能体时面临挑战：难以协调并行的证明搜索，同时保持中间命题的组织性和可靠性。

Method: Danus系统由三个核心组件构成：(1) 主智能体（main agent）负责规划与协调；(2) 多个工作智能体（worker agents）并行执行证明搜索；(3) 无状态验证器（stateless verifier）在命题被纳入事实图前进行验证。每个已验证的事实与其证明和逻辑依赖关系一同存储，主智能体定期总结演化中的证明状态，将工作智能体重定向到有前景的方向，并通过进度报告支持与人类数学家的交互。

Result: 在代数几何、奇点理论和组合学领域的六个研究级案例研究中，Danus展示了事实图内存机制使其能够构建长而详细的数学证明，验证了该编排策略对长程研究级问题的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于事实图的编排策略为扩展面向长程研究问题的数学推理智能体提供了一条有效路径。Danus已开源。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based mathematical reasoning agents have begun to tackle research-level problems and, in several cases, have contributed to the resolution of open problems. However, scaling and orchestrating such agents effectively remains challenging, due to the difficulty of coordinating parallel proof search while keeping intermediate claims organized and reliable. In this paper, we propose Danus, an orchestration system for research-level mathematical reasoning centered on a shared fact graph as a global memory-management mechanism. Danus consists of a main agent that performs planning and coordination, multiple worker agents that carry out proof search in parallel, and a stateless verifier that checks proposed mathematical claims before they are admitted into the fact graph. Each verified fact is stored together with its proof and logical dependencies, allowing the system to build long arguments incrementally while keeping the shared proof state organized. The main agent periodically summarizes the evolving proof state, redirects workers across promising directions, and supports interaction with human mathematicians through progress reports. We evaluate Danus through six research-level case studies in algebraic geometry, singularity theory, and combinatorics, illustrating how the fact-graph memory mechanism enables Danus to construct long, detailed mathematical proofs. Our results suggest that fact-graph-based orchestration provides an effective route toward scaling mathematical reasoning agents for long-horizon research problems. Danus is open source at https://github.com/frenzymath/Danus.

</details>


### [40] [A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Elastodynamic Wave Propagation in Bimaterial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06479)
*Sonal Ankush Chibire,Jenn-Terng Gau,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的框架，用于模拟双材料系统中的瞬态弹性动力波传播。以钢-铝分离式霍普金森压杆构型为例，将控制方程及初边值条件嵌入网络损失函数，并利用有限元仿真数据进行验证。该框架能精确预测波在界面处的透射与反射，且可泛化至未见时间点和修改后的材料参数，无需额外仿真，为高应变率固体力学提供了高效替代建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法（如有限元）求解双材料界面弹性动力波传播问题计算成本高，且难以提供连续可微的代理模型。PINNs有望将物理规律直接融入学习过程，为这类问题提供高效且可泛化的替代方案。

Method: 采用PINN框架，将轴对称线弹性方程、初始条件、边界条件及界面连续性条件直接编码于损失函数中；使用ANSYS Explicit Dynamics生成高保真有限元结果作为验证与补充数据约束；通过多任务损失优化网络参数。

Result: 模型在波传播、位移历史、面平均响应及主应力/应变演化上与有限元解高度吻合；能预测未见时间步和修改材料属性后的波响应；网格敏感性研究验证了数值鲁棒性，多材料组合证明了方法通用性。

Conclusion: PINN与显式有限元分析的结合为异质固体中的弹性动力波传播提供了准确且计算高效的框架，在高应变率固体力学与冲击工程领域具有重要的替代建模价值。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a promising framework for solving partial differential equations while embedding the underlying physical laws directly into the learning process. This study presents a PINN-based framework for modeling transient elastodynamic wave propagation in bimaterial systems governed by the axisymmetric equations of linear elasticity. A steel-aluminum specimen representative of a Split Hopkinson Pressure Bar configuration is considered, and the governing elastodynamic equations, together with the corresponding initial, boundary, and interface conditions, are incorporated directly into the network through a physics-informed loss function. High-fidelity finite-element simulations performed using ANSYS Workbench Explicit Dynamics are used for validation and as supplementary data constraints during training. The proposed framework accurately predicts wave transmission and reflection across the bimaterial interface and reproduces axial and radial displacement histories, face-averaged responses, and the dominant stress and strain evolution with close agreement to the finite-element solutions. The trained network further demonstrates the ability to predict wave responses at previously unseen time instants and for modified material properties without requiring additional finite-element simulations, providing a continuous surrogate model for elastodynamic analysis. Mesh-sensitivity studies confirm numerical robustness, while additional material combinations demonstrate the generality of the proposed methodology. The results show that integrating physics-informed neural networks with explicit finite-element analysis provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for elastodynamic wave propagation in heterogeneous solids, offering an effective surrogate modeling approach for high-rate solid mechanics and impact engineering applications.

</details>

Abstract: The dairy industry in Ireland has a large potential for the integration of renewable energy and the reduction of carbon emissions. However, researchers of distributed generation control are mainly focused on residential and commercial applications. To contribute to the effective integration of renewable energy in the dairy sector, this paper presents a multi-objective optimisation control system based on differential evolution and multi agent Deep Reinforcement Learning. The proposed control is organised in two layers: the upper layer uses dynamic pricing, and the lower layer is based on multi-agent reinforcement learning for battery management. This paper also simulates the electrical response of the proposed control system in a rural distribution circuit. The simulation results show that the proposed control framework can improve profits from energy arbitrage up to 18% compared to using Rule-based models, increase the use of distributed generation without significantly increasing cost, and comply with the Irish grid code in terms of voltage variation.

### [42] [Doomed from the Start: Early Abort of LLM Agent Episodes via a Recall-Controlled Probe Cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06503)
*Kai Ruan,Zihe Huang,Ziqi Zhou,Qianshan Wei,Xuan Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM内部隐藏状态表示的早期故障检测方法，通过每轮轻量级探针预测多步智能体任务是否最终失败，并构建逐轮中止级联机制，在保证高召回率的同时显著节省推理计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步LLM智能体在注定失败的任务轨迹上仍然消耗大量推理计算，直到失败变得明显才会停止。现有方法仅基于可观察行为难以在早期识别失败，因此需要从内部表征中提前检测失败信号以节省计算资源。

Method: 在每轮交互中训练轻量级探针（probe）从隐藏激活中预测任务最终是否失败；构建逐轮中止级联（abort cascade），每轮设置无分布假设的校准门控，通过联合搜索每轮召回预算保证全局召回率；仅在行为层面进行对比实验，并分析保证高召回率所需的样本复杂度。

Result: 在TextCraft上，级联在90%-97%召回目标下均达标；90%目标下Qwen-2.5-7B节省47.1%±10.3%、Llama-3.2-3B节省37.2%±8.8%的推理计算，节省量是最优单门控策略的1.6-1.7倍；仅使用行为信号的相同级联节省约一半，添加行为特征到探针无额外收益。

Conclusion: 基于隐藏状态的早期失败检测能够有效节省推理计算，且比基于行为的方法更优；隐藏状态已充分捕获行为所能揭示的信息；研究为实际部署中所需的数据量与召回率承诺之间的关系提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents solving multi-step tasks frequently commit to trajectories that are doomed to fail, yet continue to consume substantial inference compute before the failure becomes observable. We show that failure is predictable early from the agent's internal representations: lightweight per-round probes on hidden activations anticipate eventual episode failure as early as the first interaction round, where scorers reading only the agent's observable behavior are barely better than chance. We turn this signal into a practical abort cascade: one distribution-free calibrated gate per round, with per-round recall budgets jointly searched so that eventually-successful episodes survive all gates at a user-specified global rate; this episode-level guarantee is the one that matters in deployment, since false-abort risk accumulates across gates. Across two agent models on TextCraft, the cascade meets every recall target from 90% to 97% and, at the 90% target, saves 47.1% +/- 10.3% (Qwen-2.5-7B) and 37.2% +/- 8.8% (Llama-3.2-3B) of inference compute, 1.6--1.7x the best single-gate policy. An otherwise-identical cascade reading only behavior saves roughly half as much, and adding behavioral features to the probe yields no further gain: the hidden states capture what behavior reveals. Finally, we characterize the sample complexity of certifying high recall targets, telling practitioners which recall promises their data can, and provably cannot, back. The code will be released soon.

</details>

*Qian Sun,Yong-Ming Tian,Jia-Wei Huang,Cheng Feng,Shao-Qun Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模真实多变量时间序列语料库RMISC（约200个数据集、1420亿时间点），并基于此预训练了四个先进的时间序列基础模型。实验表明，使用真实多变量数据训练相比合成数据能显著提升模型的零样本泛化能力，为开发更强的时间序列基础模型提供了重要指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要在多变量合成数据上预训练，合成数据虽易于扩展但可能无法捕捉真实时间序列中复杂的时序动态和跨变量关系。因此需要探究：基于真实语料库训练的领先TSFM相比合成数据训练的模型究竟能带来多大性能提升？

Method: 1) 建立RMISC语料库——大规模、高质量、开放获取的真实多变量时间序列档案，涵盖约200个数据集和1420亿时间点；2) 在单变量、合成多变量和真实多变量三种数据上预训练四个先进TSFM；3) 在标准分布内和分布外基准上评估零样本泛化能力。

Result: 纳入真实多变量数据能显著提升单变量和多变量TSFM的零样本泛化性能，效果优于合成多变量数据训练的模型。

Conclusion: 真实多变量数据对发展更强的时间序列基础模型具有重要贡献，验证了真实数据相较于合成数据的优势，为未来TSFM的预训练数据选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the emergence of multivariate modeling using time series foundation models (TSFMs), which achieve advanced zero-shot generalization. Modern multivariate TSFMs are predominantly pretrained on multivariate synthetic data, which is easier to scale but may fail to capture the complex temporal dynamics and cross-variable relationships present in real-world time series. This raises a key question: Whether and to what extent the leading TSFMs trained with the real-world corpus perform better than those trained with synthetic data? To answer this, we establish the RMISC corpus, a considerably large-scale, high-quality, openly accessible, real-world, and multivariate time series archive that contains around 200 datasets and 142 billion time points across diverse domains. Furthermore, we pretrain four advanced TSFMs on univariate, synthetic multivariate, and real-world multivariate data and evaluate their zero-shot generalization capabilities on standard in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks. Experimental results show that incorporating real-world multivariate data predominantly improves the generalization performance for both univariate and multivariate TSFMs. These results provide a deeper understanding of how real-world multivariate data contributes to the development of stronger TSFMs.

</details>

Motivation: 现有强化学习环境在捕捉格斗游戏核心策略（即循环、非传递的中立博弈互动）方面要么过于复杂难以分析，要么过于简单失去非平凡性，缺乏一个平衡可分析性与复杂度的标准化测试平台。

Abstract: We present FootsiesGym, an open-source environment for learning in a non-trivial two-player, zero-sum, imperfect-information game. Built on HiFight's minimalist 2D fighting game Footsies, it isolates the cyclic, non-transitive strategic interactions of fighting game neutral play while remaining simple enough for efficient analysis. We provide a vectorized simulator that enables high-throughput training on standard hardware, making the environment accessible and reproducible. We describe the design of the environment, benchmark several reinforcement learning algorithms, and discuss open research directions it enables. The code is available at https://github.com/como-research/FootsiesGym.

</details>


### [45] [FreqDepthKV: Frequency-Guided Depth Sharing for Robust KV Cache Compression in Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06519)
*Anna Córdoba,Adam Puente Tercero,Nerea Angulo Hijo,Mar Linares Tercero,Julia Barrientos,Ainhoa Miranda,Jesús Olivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FreqDepthKV提出了一种推理时KV缓存压缩方法，通过将相邻层KV状态分解为共享低频深度分量和稀疏高频残差，并利用轻量级在线探头动态分配注意力头到不同缓存模式，实现了在显著降低缓存预算下保持任务精度，达到3.9倍有效压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于KV缓存的内存和带宽开销，而激进的压缩会丢失检索和多步推理所需的逐层证据。

Method: 将相邻层KV状态因式分解为共享的低频深度分量和稀疏的高频残差；使用轻量级在线探头根据注意力头对重建敏感注意力logits的贡献，动态分配头到共享深度、残差深度或精确缓存三种模式。

Result: 在32k-token预填充窗口下，Exact Match达58.3，F1达63.0，ROUGE-L达32.5，pass@1达48.1；解码吞吐量提升至70.4 tokens/s，TTFT降至2.06秒，峰值KV内存降至6.2 GB，实现3.9倍有效压缩比。

Conclusion: FreqDepthKV能在大幅压缩缓存的同时保持任务精度，在长上下文问答、检索、摘要和代码生成等基准测试中均优于先前压缩缓存方法。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is increasingly limited by the memory and bandwidth cost of KV caches, yet aggressive compression can remove the layer-specific evidence needed for retrieval and multi-step reasoning. We introduce FreqDepthKV, an inference-time cache compression method that factorizes adjacent-layer KV states into shared low-frequency depth components and sparse high-frequency residuals. A lightweight online probe assigns attention heads to shared-depth, residual-depth, or exact cache modes according to their contribution to reconstruction-sensitive attention logits, allowing the compression policy to adapt to prompt structure without retraining. Across long-context question answering, needle retrieval, summarization, and code generation benchmarks, FreqDepthKV preserves task accuracy under substantially smaller cache budgets. With a 32k-token prefill window, FreqDepthKV reaches 58.3 Exact Match, 63.0 F1, 32.5 ROUGE-L, and 48.1 pass@1, closely matching full KV while outperforming prior compressed-cache methods. It also improves decoding throughput to 70.4 tokens/s, reduces TTFT to 2.06 seconds, and lowers peak KV memory to 6.2 GB, achieving a 3.9x effective compression ratio.

</details>


### [46] [Bridging Physical Reasoning and Task Generalization via Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06522)
*Han-Jun Ko,Jr-Jen Chen,Haobo Yuan,Hsin-Ying Lee,Tiancheng Shen,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VAORA框架，通过两种对齐奖励（视觉对齐奖励和视觉-行为对齐奖励）解决VLM在交互式物理推理中的幻觉链式思考和推理-行为错位问题，在PHYRE和Virtual Tool基准上取得跨任务和环境泛化的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在交互式物理推理中泛化能力差，存在两个关键失败模式：产生与物理现实矛盾的幻觉链式思考（hallucinated CoT），以及模型推理与实际行动之间的错位（misalignment）。现有方法未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出VAORA奖励设计框架，包含两个互补奖励：1) 视觉对齐奖励（Visual Alignment Reward），将VLM推理锚定到视觉上下文，独立于智能体动作；2) 视觉-行为对齐奖励（Visual-Action Alignment Reward），基于模型动作诱导的视觉结果来 grounding 推理。为提高训练稳定性，使用预训练领域专家智能体估计成功概率，实现平滑密集奖励。

Result: 在PHYRE和Virtual Tool基准上，VAORA在新型任务和未见环境设定下均取得了性能提升，验证了该方法能够诱导出可泛化的物理智能。

Conclusion: 通过VAORA对推理过程进行视觉和行为层面的双重对齐约束，可以有效抑制幻觉CoT并缩小推理与行为之间的差距，从而提升VLM在交互式物理推理中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to generalize in interactive physical reasoning, particularly under unseen tasks and environments. Two key failure modes are prominent: hallucinated chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that contradicts physical reality, and misalignment between the model's reasoning and actions. We present VAORA (Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment), a novel reward design that directly addresses both issues. VAORA introduces two complementary rewards: Visual Alignment Reward, which anchors VLM reasoning to the visual context independent of the agent action itself, and Visual-Action Alignment Reward, which grounds reasoning in the visual outcome induced by the model's action. Together, these rewards suppress hallucinated CoT and reduce the gap between reasoning and behavior. To improve training stability, we further employ smooth, dense rewards by estimating success probabilities using a pre-trained in-domain expert agent. Experiments on PHYRE and Virtual Tool support our performances across novel-task and unseen-environment settings, confirming that grounded and generalizable physical intelligence can be induced through VAORA.

</details>


### [47] [DepthWeave-KV: Token-Adaptive Cross-Layer Residual Factorization for Long-Context KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06523)
*Anna Cordoba,Adam Puente Tercero,Nerea Angulo Hijo,Mar Linares Tercero,Julia Barrientos,Ainhoa Miranda,Jesus Olivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DepthWeave-KV是一种token自适应的键值缓存压缩方法，通过跨层低秩分解和token条件路由，在保持任务质量的同时实现8.3倍KV内存压缩和72.8 tokens/s的解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型推理受限于键值缓存的内存带宽和容量，现有压缩方法在层或token上使用统一预算，当词汇线索和语义状态需要不同保留程度时会导致检索性能下降。

Method: (1) 使用共享低秩通道基对相邻transformer层的键值状态进行跨层残差分解；(2) 保留轻量级token特定残差以处理注意力敏感区域；(3) 采用token条件深度路由器，为指令承载和检索关键token分配更高重建秩；(4) 利用注意力输出探针进行免校准在线误差追踪，在生成过程中自适应调整压缩。

Result: 在LongBench、Needle-in-a-Haystack、L-Eval及长形式QA和摘要基准上，DepthWeave-KV接近全缓存任务质量，显著降低内存使用。平均分数和检索准确率优于先前的压缩缓存方法，在64K上下文下实现8.3倍KV内存压缩和72.8 tokens/s的解码速度。

Conclusion: DepthWeave-KV通过token自适应跨层压缩和在线误差追踪，有效解决了长上下文推理中内存瓶颈与检索质量之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Long-context language model inference is increasingly limited by the memory bandwidth and capacity required to store key-value caches, yet existing compression methods often apply uniform budgets across layers or tokens and degrade retrieval when lexical cues and semantic states require different preservation. We introduce DepthWeave-KV, a token-adaptive cache compression method that factorizes key and value states across neighboring transformer layers using shared low-rank channel bases while retaining lightweight token-specific residuals where attention behavior is sensitive. DepthWeave-KV combines cross-depth residual factorization with a token-conditional depth router that allocates higher reconstruction rank to instruction-bearing and retrieval-critical tokens, and uses calibration-free online error tracking from attention-output probes to adapt compression during generation without retraining the base model. A fused CUDA implementation jointly performs basis lookup, residual dequantization, and attention projection to reduce decode-time memory traffic. Across LongBench, Needle-in-a-Haystack, L-Eval, and long-form QA and summarization benchmarks, DepthWeave-KV achieves near-full-cache task quality with substantially lower memory use, improving average score and retrieval accuracy over prior compressed caches while reaching 8.3x KV memory reduction and 72.8 tokens per second at 64K context.

</details>


### [48] [The Large Cancer Assistant (LCA): A Model-Agnostic Orchestration Framework for Scalable Clinical Decision Support in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06531)
*Ghassen Marrakchi,Basarab Matei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大癌症助手(LCA)，一个模型无关、事后编排框架，通过算法不可渗透性原理和7元组架构解耦多模态数据摄入与AI推理，实现可扩展的临床决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前肿瘤学多模态深度学习模型采用单体式设计，将数据摄入、临床路由和AI推理刚性耦合，缺乏灵活性。LCA旨在通过模型无关的事后编排框架解决这一问题。

Method: LCA形式化为基于算法不可渗透性原理的7元组架构；引入基于几何深度学习的入口理论标准化多模态数据；通过癌症切换模块动态编排数据；通过标准化中间载荷隔离核心AI执行与医院IT基础设施。

Result: PoC在四个技术场景中验证了编排逻辑：名义流程开销可忽略；AI模型替换时路由投影不变，验证了算法不可渗透性；数据异常下SDR生成召回率100%，验证了故障安全性；多协议执行能力得到验证。

Conclusion: LCA通过结构性解耦多模态摄入与特征推理，提供了高度适应性和模块化的编排基础。SIP建立了清晰的架构边界，为下游EMR互操作性奠定了独立范式基础。

Abstract: - Objective: Multimodal deep learning models in oncology are currently limited by monolithic designs that rigidly couple data ingestion, clinical routing, and artificial intelligence (AI) inference. To address this inflexibility, we propose the Large Cancer Assistant (LCA), a model-agnostic, post-hoc orchestration framework designed for scalable clinical decision support. - Methods: The LCA is mathematically formalized as a 7-tuple architecture grounded in the principle of Algorithmic Impermeability, ensuring the orchestration logic remains strictly independent of underlying black-box AI models. We introduce the Entry Theory, leveraging Geometric Deep Learning (GDL) to standardize multimodal patient data along distinct structural and medical axes. The system dynamically orchestrates data via a Cancer Switching Module and intentionally isolates the core AI execution from volatile hospital IT infrastructures by outputting a Standardized Intermediate Payload (SIP). - Results: A Proof of Concept (PoC) validated the orchestration logic across four technical scenarios. The framework executed a nominal flow with negligible orchestration overhead. It empirically demonstrated algorithmic impermeability by maintaining an invariant routing projection during AI model swaps, and it validated strict failure-safety by achieving a 100\% recall rate in generating targeted Supplementary Data Requests (SDR) under injected data anomalies. Multi-protocol execution capability was also successfully verified. - Conclusion: By structurally decoupling multimodal ingestion from feature inference, the LCA provides a highly adaptable and modular orchestration foundation. The SIP establishes a clear architectural boundary, natively setting the stage for downstream Electronic Medical Record (EMR) interoperability as an independent future paradigm.

</details>


### [49] [Rethinking Indic AI from a Lens of Cultural Heritage Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06544)
*Aparna Madva,Sharath Srivatsa,Srinath Srinivasa,Tulika Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了印度次大陆AI发展中语言与文化的双刃剑效应，梳理了印地语自然语言处理（Indic NLP）的历史演进、结构与社会语言学挑战，分析了印地语基础模型的进展，并提出了基于诠释学推理的"文化感知"（Culture Sensing）研究方向，旨在解决低资源语言公平性和文化意义输出等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在印度次大陆的快速发展带来了语言与文化层面的双重影响：一方面促进包容与可及性，另一方面可能导致世界观同质化及对 underrepresented 语言和文化的排斥。本文旨在系统刻画这一问题，为构建更具包容性和稳健性的印地语基础模型提供方向性指导。

Method: 采用纵向综述方法，梳理Indic NLP的历史发展脉络（关键里程碑、方法论转变、资源构建）；分析印度语言的结构与社会语言学特征（丰富形态、复杂文字与语法、双言现象、方言变异）；讨论印地语基础模型在解决资源与表征差距方面的作用；提出"文化感知"研究框架。

Result: 识别了印度语言在构建AI基础模型时面临的独特挑战（形态学复杂性、正字法多样、双言现象、方言差异等）；总结了Indic NLP从早期资源建设到当前基础模型的发展轨迹；论证了现有基础模型在覆盖低资源语言和文化表征方面的不足。

Conclusion: 提出"文化感知"（Culture Sensing）这一基于诠释学推理的研究方向，以重新构想AI系统；指出未来研究应聚焦于低资源语言的公平性能表现和文化意义上的输出生成，为下一阶段Indic NLP研究提供了路线图。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI) makes inroads into different parts of the Indian subcontinent, there is significant interest in studying how AI impacts the linguistic and cultural foundations of this civilization. AI is seen as a ''double-edged sword'' where on the one hand, it can enable access and inclusion for a large population, on the other, it can homogenize worldviews and exclude underrepresented languages and worldviews. In this paper, we try to characterize this problem by addressing the extensive characteristic nature of Indian linguistics and the way they closely connect to cultural practices and worldview. We then perform a longitudinal survey of how Natural Language Processing (NLP) techniques have evolved in this space, tracing the historical development of Indic NLP, covering key milestones, methodological shifts, and resource creation efforts. In addition, the paper also examines the structural and sociolinguistic characteristics of Indian languages, such as rich morphology, complex scripts and grammar rules, diglossia, and large dialectal variation, and explains how these create unique challenges for building AI foundation models. We then discuss the growing role of Indic foundation models and analyze how these models address these long-standing resource and representation gaps. Finally, we propose a research direction called 'Culture Sensing', which re-imagines AI based on hermeneutic reasoning. Culture Sensing aims to address open problems such as ensuring equitable performance across low-resource languages and producing outputs that are culturally meaningful. By bringing together past work, current techniques, and emerging trends, this paper outlines research directions that can guide the next phase of Indic NLP and contribute to the development of more robust and inclusive Indic foundation models.

</details>

### [50] [CanvasAgent: Enabling Complex Image Creation and Editing via Visual Tool Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05465)
*Hairui Zhu,Yiying Yang,Tengjin Weng,Ziyu Lu,Xiao Yao,Xiaoyang Ye,Lin Ma,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CanvasCraft数据集（140K可执行轨迹+10K RL任务规约）和CanvasAgent多模态智能体，通过SFT+GRPO混合奖励训练，实现复杂图像创建和编辑中的多工具协同调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态工具智能体主要优化感知、搜索或特定领域编辑，缺乏面向可执行图像创建轨迹的大规模监督数据，难以应对需要多种视觉工具协同操作的复杂图像创建和编辑任务。

Method: 构建CanvasCraft数据集（140K注释可执行轨迹+10K RL任务规约）；提出CanvasAgent多模态智能体，先通过SFT学习可执行推理-行动轨迹，再使用GRPO结合结果级和过程级混合奖励进行优化，在交互过程中检查中间结果、追踪视觉资产并适应动态视觉状态。

Result: 通过最终图像质量和轨迹行为的实验评估，验证了CanvasAgent和CanvasCraft数据集在复杂多工具图像创建工作流中的有效性。

Conclusion: CanvasAgent能够有效编排异构视觉工具进行多轮交互，CanvasCraft为复杂图像创建提供了大规模监督数据，推动了多模态智能体从感知增强推理向操作中心视觉创建的转变。

Abstract: Complex image creation and editing often require more than a single generation or editing model. A user request may involve synthesizing images, localizing objects, segmenting regions, editing selected content, compositing intermediate assets, reading text, and enhancing the final result. Such tasks shift multimodal agents from perception-augmented reasoning to manipulation-centered visual creation, where tools must actively transform visual states rather than merely inspect them. However, existing multimodal tool-use agents are mostly optimized for perception, search, or domain-specific editing, and lack large-scale supervision for executable image-creation trajectories. In this paper, we introduce CanvasCraft, a large-scale multimodal tool-use dataset for complex image creation and editing, and \textbf{CanvasAgent}, a tool-augmented multimodal agent that learns to orchestrate heterogeneous visual tools through multi-turn interaction. CanvasCraft contains 140K fully annotated executable trajectories and 10K
  RL task specifications. CanvasAgent is first trained with SFT to learn executable reasoning-action trajectories, and is then optimized with GRPO using a hybrid reward that combines outcome- and process-level signals. During rollout, CanvasAgent inspects intermediate results, tracks visual assets, and adapts tool decisions to the evolving visual state. Experiments evaluate both final image quality and trajectory behavior, demonstrating the effectiveness of CanvasAgent and the proposed dataset for complex multi-tool image creation workflows.

</details>


### [51] [A Task-Driven Evaluation of UAV Detection and Tracking under Synthetic Fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05467)
*Amir Pouladi,Vesal Ahsani,Haijun Li,Homayoun Najjaran,Afzal Suleman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务驱动的评估框架，将深度感知合成雾生成、图像恢复、目标检测与跟踪集成到统一流程中，评估雾霾对无人机小目标检测与跟踪的影响。研究发现雾霾主要通过增加漏检来降低性能，含雾训练最为鲁棒，测试时恢复仅在清洁训练数据下有效，且图像恢复质量并不线性转化为下游感知性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在天空主导、远距离成像场景中，雾霾严重降低可见度；真实雾霾场景数据采集与标注困难，缺乏将图像恢复与下游检测跟踪联合评估的系统性研究。

Method: 利用单目深度估计与大气散射模型从真实晴天图像生成合成雾数据；比较经典、CNN和Transformer三类图像恢复方法；将恢复模型集成到检测-跟踪流程；分别在仅清洁训练和含雾训练两种模式下评估多种检测器，并在清洁、雾霾和恢复视频序列上评估基于检测的跟踪性能。

Result: 雾霾通过增加漏检显著降低检测与跟踪性能；含雾训练提供最一致鲁棒性提升；测试时恢复仅在清洁训练数据下有显著收益；图像恢复质量与下游感知性能提升不成比例。

Conclusion: 图像恢复质量并不必然转化为下游感知的成比例增益，恢复方法应与检测和跟踪性能联合评估，而非仅以图像级指标衡量。

Abstract: Fog severely degrades the visibility of small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in skydominant, long-range imagery, reducing the reliability of downstream detection and tracking. This paper presents a task-driven evaluation framework that links depth-aware synthetic fog generation, image restoration, object detection, and tracking within a unified pipeline. Given the practical difficulty of collecting and annotating foggy UAV scenes, synthetic fog is generated from real clear-weather outdoor images containing UAV targets using monocular depth estimation and the atmospheric scattering model. Representative restoration methods from classical, convolutional neural network (CNN)-based, and transformer-based families are first compared, after which the selected restoration model is integrated into the downstream perception pipeline. Detection is evaluated under both clean-only and fog-inclusive training regimes using multiple detector variants, while tracking-by-detection is assessed on clean, foggy, and restored video sequences. Beyond image-level restoration metrics, the study evaluates how fog and restoration affect detection robustness and tracking performance. The results show that fog substantially degrades both detection and tracking, primarily through increased missed detections. Fog-inclusive training provides the most consistent improvement in robustness, whereas test-time restoration is most beneficial when the detector has been trained only on clean imagery. These findings show that restoration quality does not necessarily translate into proportional gains in downstream perception and therefore should be evaluated jointly with detection and tracking performance.

</details>


### [52] [Binocular Gaze Estimation with Single Camera and Single Light Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05473)
*Tongbing Huang,Yang Fu,Yunfei Wang,Zhaocan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用单摄像头和单光源的视线估计方法，通过引入"虚拟光源"概念，利用瞳孔间距与光斑间的关系估计"虚拟光斑"，配合多项式回归实现头部自由移动下的视线追踪，性能虽有下降但可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备等场景中，传统视线追踪系统需要至少一个摄像头和两个光源，组件过多不利于集成。为减少硬件组件（尤其是光源数量），提出单摄像头单光源方案。

Method: 1) 引入与真实光源关于摄像头对称的"虚拟光源"，生成"虚拟光斑"；2) 利用图像中两瞳孔间距与两光斑间距的关系估计虚拟光斑位置；3) 假设存在两个光源，使用多项式回归进行视线估计；4) 提出针对单光斑系统的归一化因子。

Result: 该单光源系统的性能可被接受，但与双光源系统相比存在性能下降。

Conclusion: 所提方法在减少硬件组件的前提下实现了可用的视线估计，验证了单光源系统的可行性，为移动设备等资源受限场景提供了实用方案。

Abstract: According to commonly consented theories, the minimum hardware requirement for gaze tracker is one camera and two light sources to realize gaze estimation with free head movements. However, in some scenarios such as eye tracking on mobile devices, it is preferable to use less components, especially light sources. We propose a gaze estimation method with one camera and one light source. A "virtual light source" is introduced, which is geometrically placed symmetrically to the real light source with respect to the camera, and generates a "virtual glint" in the acquired image. We estimate the "virtual glint" by exploiting the relationship between the distance between two pupils and two glints in the captured image, and estimate the gaze with polynomial regression assuming two light sources are available. A new normalization factor for regression method is verified, which turns out to be practical for one-glint system. The performance is proved to be acceptable, while degradation is noticed compared to system with two actual light sources.

</details>


### [53] [Ground3D-LMM: Fine-Grained 3D Point Grounding and Spatial Reasoning with LMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05493)
*Amol Harsh,Zongyan Han,Jean Lahoud,Ye Liu,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ground3D-LMM，一个统一的三维大多模态模型，支持基于点云和RGB图像的可验证、度量感知的空间对话，能够输出带有点级3D定位和以真实世界单位计量的数值响应（如尺寸、厚度、间距等），并在ScanNet/ScanNet++上构建了约250万问答对的大规模数据集，在多项任务上建立了强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D大模型要么输出无明确3D定位的对话响应，要么不具备交互式度量感知能力，无法同时满足响应的可验证性（显式定位到3D区域）和度量性（以真实世界单位输出物理测量值）。

Method: 提出Ground3D-LMM统一模型，以点云和可选的RGB图像为输入，支持3D空间对话，能生成带有点级定位的响应和以真实世界单位计量的度量数值输出（涵盖物体级和部件级粒度，以及多物体查询）。同时定义了3D接地度量任务，并基于ScanNet和ScanNet++构建了包含密集物体/部件标注和约250万问答对的大规模数据集。

Result: 在多个数据集和任务上的大量实验表明，Ground3D-LMM为可验证、度量感知的3D对话理解提供了强基线。

Conclusion: Ground3D-LMM有效统一了3D定位与度量感知对话，填补了现有方法的空白，数据集和模型均已公开。

Abstract: Natural-language queries about 3D environments become actionable when responses are verifiable and metric. Verifiability requires explicit grounding to the referred 3D region, while metric answers report physical measurements in real-world units (e.g., size, thickness, clearance, and distance). Existing 3D large multimodal models (LMMs) approaches remain limited: conversational systems typically respond without explicit 3D grounding, while 3D grounding models are not designed for interactive, metric-aware dialogue. In this paper, we present Ground3D-LMM, a unified model that takes a point cloud and an optional RGB image as input and supports 3D spatial conversation with (i) point-grounded responses and (ii) metric numeric outputs at both object and part granularity, including multi-object queries. To evaluate this intersection of grounding and measurement, we define the 3D Grounded Measurement task, which requires predicting the referred 3D region and the corresponding metric quantities in real-world units. We introduce a large-scale dataset built on ScanNet and ScanNet++ datasets with dense object and part annotations and roughly 2.5M question-answer pairs spanning eight tasks, along with a manually verified test set. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets and tasks show that our proposed Ground3D-LMM model provides a strong baseline for grounded, metric-aware 3D conversational understanding. Our dataset and model are publicly available.

</details>


### [54] [Light-Omni: Reflex over Reasoning in Agentic Video Understanding with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05511)
*Chang Nie,Jiaju Wei,Junlan Feng,Chaoyou Fu,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Light-Omni 提出了一种双上下文状态机制（全局状态+参数化潜状态），在单次前向传播中构建全局上下文，避免侦探式迭代推理，实现轻量级响应式视频理解。在多个基准上以2.4%精度提升、12.1倍加速和2.6倍GPU内存效率优势超越M3-Agent，并可作为现有MLLM的记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频智能体依赖侦探式迭代推理（如search）进行动作控制和证据聚合，导致高昂成本和延迟。这种繁重推理主要是为了弥补全局上下文的缺失和检索中的语义对齐不足。

Method: 提出Light-Omni框架，核心是双上下文状态：1）全局状态：从情景记忆中持续整合的有限大小多模态脚本，通过层次化合并保留近期细节并总结过去事件；2）参数化潜状态：以全局状态为条件生成，直接驱动自主动作并产生检索嵌入，延迟最小化。耦合设计实现语义对齐的检索和响应式响应，避免迭代推理。

Result: 在多个视频基准上，Light-Omni以平均2.4%精度提升、12.1倍加速和2.6倍GPU内存效率优势超越M3-Agent，并能作为记忆系统提升现有MLLM的性能和效率。

Conclusion: 双上下文状态机制有效解决了视频理解中全局上下文缺失和语义对齐问题，在保持高性能的同时显著降低延迟和资源消耗。

Abstract: Agentic video understanding equips models with long-term memory to autonomously process and respond to continuous, long-horizon multimodal streams. However, advanced video agents often rely on ``detective-style'' iterative reasoning for action control (e.g., $\mathtt{search}$) and evidence aggregation, incurring prohibitive costs and latency. We argue that such heavy reasoning primarily compensates for the lack of global context and semantic misalignment in retrieval. This paper introduces Light-Omni, a multimodal agent framework for reflexive and lightweight video understanding. It achieves this through dual contextual states that instantly build the required context in a single forward pass. First, we maintain a global state, a finite-sized multimodal script continuously consolidated from episodic memory, serving as the global context for Light-Omni. Through hierarchical merging, it preserves recent details while summarizing past events. Second, conditioned on this global context, we generate a parametric latent state that directly drives autonomous actions and produces retrieval embeddings, with minimal latency. Benefiting from this coupled design, Light-Omni achieves semantically aligned retrieval and reflexive responses while avoiding iterative reasoning. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of Light-Omni across multiple video benchmarks. Notably, it outperforms M3-Agent with an average 2.4% accuracy gain, a 12.1$\times$ speedup, and a 2.6$\times$ improvement in GPU memory efficiency. Furthermore, it serves as a memory system to enhance both the performance and efficiency of existing MLLMs. Project page: https://clare-nie.github.io/Light-Omni.

</details>


### [55] [Statistical Adversaries: Natural Backdoor-like Features in Vision Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05516)
*Paul K. Mandal,Pavan Reddy,Tristan Malatynski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉数据中自然存在的统计信号（非恶意插入）可像后门触发器一样操纵模型预测，这些信号比通用噪声更具针对性且可跨架构迁移，表明数据集自身的统计结构构成了潜在攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注模型特定的对抗攻击，但忽略了数据集中自然存在的统计模式是否本身就能对模型产生类似后门的破坏性影响。

Method: 在ImageNet上分析与特定标签强相关的统计模式，通过统计控制去除随机相关性，并验证这些信号对模型预测的直接和可预测影响。

Result: 这些"统计对抗样本"比通用噪声更具针对性，能够跨不同模型架构迁移，且其效果直接源于数据集结构与分布，而非单个模型的特性。

Conclusion: 常规数据集中存在可利用的对抗表面，即使没有投毒攻击也存在。数据集审计应将虚假结构不仅视为偏差或可解释性问题，还应视为视觉模型的潜在攻击面。

Abstract: Model-specific adversarial attacks have been extensively studied. We study a different failure mode: naturally occurring statistical signals in vision data that can behave like backdoor-like triggers without being maliciously inserted. We call these signals statistical adversaries. We analyse Imagenet to find patterns that are strongly linked to certain labels. We then use statistical controls to remove random correlations from our candidate signals. Finally, we demonstrate that these signals directly and predictably alter model predictions. These statistical adversaries are more targeted than generic corruptions and transfer across different model architectures. This suggests that some vulnerabilities are driven by dataset structure and distribution rather than a single model's idiosyncrasies. We conclude that ordinary datasets can contain exploitable adversarial surfaces even in the absence of poisoning, and suggest that dataset audits should treat spurious structure not only as a source of bias or interpretability failure, but also as a latent attack surface for vision models.

</details>


### [56] [Rendering-Aware Bayesian 3D Gaussian Splatting with Native Uncertainty and Adaptive Complexity Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05522)
*Gaoxiang Jia,Vikram Appia,Junzhou Huang,Xinlei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种渲染感知的贝叶斯3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，通过正态逆 Wishart（NIW）后验对高斯均值与协方差进行概率建模，结合可选的狄利克雷过程扩展实现组件使用信号。在主动视角选择任务中，NIW本征采集优于标准集成基线（PSNR +0.453 dB, LPIPS -0.0146），且NIW本征区间相比共享代理将95%覆盖误差降低约17倍，训练成本约为深度集成的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS训练依赖点估计和手工启发式规则，缺乏不确定性量化和复杂度控制，在稀疏视图或固定采集预算场景下难以识别弱支持几何和选择信息性视角。

Method: 提出渲染感知的贝叶斯3DGS框架，使用正态逆Wishart（NIW）后验对高斯均值与协方差建模，通过渲染器导出的替代摘要进行推断；可选狄利克雷过程扩展提供概率性组件使用信号；显式区分闭式解与近似推断边界。

Result: 在16→32视角主动采集任务中，NIW本征采集PSNR提升+0.453 dB、LPIPS降低-0.0146，优于标准集成基线；NIW本征区间95%覆盖误差0.046（共享代理0.796，深度集成0.454），训练成本约为深度集成的1/3；39组场景-种子实验中PSNR +0.030 dB，训练时间仅增加1.6%。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯3DGS可作为面向决策任务（如主动视角选择）的实用概率场景表示，在提供可靠不确定性量化的同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is a strong representation for real-time novel-view synthesis, but its standard training pipeline relies on point estimates and hand-tuned heuristics, providing no native uncertainty or principled complexity control. This is most limiting under sparse views or fixed acquisition budgets, where a model must identify weakly supported geometry and select informative views. We introduce a rendering-aware Bayesian 3DGS framework that tracks Gaussian geometry with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart posterior over means and covariances using renderer-derived surrogate summaries. An optional Dirichlet-process extension adds a probabilistic component-usage signal, and the training schedule makes the closed-form versus approximate inference boundary explicit. Re-rendering posterior geometry samples yields native predictive uncertainty for interval calibration and active view selection. In a fixed-budget 16-to-32 active-view task, native NIW acquisition improves PSNR by +0.453 dB and LPIPS by -0.0146 over a scoring-only 3-member standard-ensemble baseline, winning 29/39 scene-seed pairs and 10/13 scene means; it also improves over PPU-style (+0.355 dB) and NIW-proxy (+0.401 dB) acquisition. NIW native intervals reduce 95% coverage error by about 17x relative to a shared proxy (0.046 vs. 0.796) and are about 10x closer to nominal coverage than a 3-member deep ensemble (0.047 vs. 0.454) at roughly one-third the training cost. As a reconstruction compatibility check, paired NIW-vs-standard analysis over 39 scene-seed runs yields +0.030 dB PSNR with 1.6% additional training time. These results position Bayesian 3DGS as a practical probabilistic scene representation for decision-facing tasks such as active view selection.

</details>


### [57] [Multi-Teacher Contrastive Distillation for Edge-Efficient Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05533)
*Tim Lenz,Maurice Heide,Marco Gustav,Nic G. Reitsam,Jakob Nikolas Kather*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuCoDi是一个预训练框架，通过从多个计算病理学基础模型（Virchow2、UNI2、H-Optimus-1）中蒸馏知识到轻量级MobileOne和RepViT学生编码器，实现了在保持接近教师模型性能的同时将模型规模缩减数个数量级，并在树莓派5上获得高达605倍的单切片速度提升，推动了病理学基础模型向边缘设备部署的进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理学基础模型（PFMs）规模庞大、推理成本高，难以在病理科本地部署，需要在保持高性能的同时大幅压缩模型以适应边缘设备。

Method: 提出MuCoDi框架，采用对比蒸馏目标（改编自MoCo v3），将多个冻结的PFM（Virchow2、UNI2、H-Optimus-1）的切片嵌入作为替换动量编码器键的缓存，训练轻量级MobileOne和RepViT学生模型。在14.3M TCGA切片（仅11.8K WSI）上预训练，并在23个临床下游分类任务上评估冻结编码器。

Result: RepViT-based MuCoEdge：R2.3和R1.5达到71.0%外部AUROC（离最佳教师Virchow2的71.8%仅差0.8个百分点）；R2.3获得最佳外部F1（51.8%）和次佳AUPRC（53.3%）；R1.0仅6.4M参数/1.12 GFLOPs即达70.9% AUROC。MobileOne在树莓派5上获得最高605倍单切片加速，外部AUROC保持在66.5%-66.9%。

Conclusion: 证明了通过多教师对比蒸馏可以将PFM级别的病理学表示压缩到极轻量级模型，实现接近教师性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，推动病理学AI向实际边缘部署场景迈进。

Abstract: Computational pathology foundation models (PFMs) have advanced whole-slide image analysis. However, their size and inference cost hinder local deployment in pathology departments. We propose MuCoDi, a pretraining framework that distills frozen tile embeddings from multiple PFMs into compact edge-oriented encoders. Instead of regressing individual teacher features, MuCoDi trains lightweight MobileOne and RepViT students with a contrastive distillation objective adapted from MoCo v3, where cached Virchow2, UNI2, and H-Optimus-1 embeddings replace momentum-encoder keys. We pretrain students on 14.3M TCGA tiles from only 11.8K WSIs and evaluate frozen encoders on 23 clinically curated downstream classification tasks. RepViT-based MuCoEdge students retain near-teacher performance while reducing model size by orders of magnitude: MuCoEdge-R2.3 and MuCoEdge-R1.5 reach 71.0% external AUROC, within 0.8 percentage points of the best teacher (Virchow2, 71.8%), while MuCoEdge-R2.3 obtains the best external F1 and the second-best AUPRC (51.8% and 53.3%). MuCoEdge-R1.0 reaches 70.9% AUROC with only 6.4M parameters and 1.12 GFLOPs. On a Raspberry Pi 5, sub-million-parameter MobileOne students achieve up to 605-fold single-tile speedup over Virchow2 while retaining 66.5% to 66.9% external AUROC, demonstrating that PFM-quality pathology representations can be moved toward practical edge deployment. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mucodi-6243.

</details>


### [58] [Harnessing Generative Image Models for Training-Free Primitive Shape Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05568)
*Gregor Kobsik,Tim Elsner,Leif Kobbelt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的零样本框架，通过多视图渲染、视觉语言模型分析语义部件、生成图像模型绘制分割掩码、重投影到几何体并拟合超二次曲面基元，实现了对3D形状的紧凑基元表示。该方法在HumanPrim和Toys4K数据集上以5-9个基元/物体取得最低Chamfer距离，且具有类别无关和旋转不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式图像模型虽具备通用视觉理解能力，但将其用于下游任务通常需要微调；本文探索是否可以不经过任何训练直接利用其预训练能力来实现3D形状的基元分解。

Method: 多视图渲染3D物体→视觉语言模型分析语义部件→生成图像模型绘制颜色编码的分割掩码→重投影到几何体→参数优化拟合超二次曲面基元。整个流程不含任何可学习参数。

Result: 在HumanPrim和Toys4K数据集上，使用平均5-9个基元/物体，取得了所有评估方法中最低的Chamfer距离。部件分割（而非基元拟合）是当前精度的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生成式预训练模型的能力可以直接用于3D基元分解而无需额外训练，且随生成模型改进精度天花板会进一步提升。该方法实现了类别无关和旋转不变性，克服了以往学习方法的局限。

Abstract: Representing 3D shapes as compact sets of geometric primitives is fundamental to robotics, simulation, and scene understanding. Generative image models trained at scale have recently emerged as generalist visual learners that can identify and segment object parts directly in the image domain, across arbitrary categories and without task-specific training. Adapting such models to downstream tasks typically requires fine-tuning; we ask whether their pretrained capability can instead be harnessed directly, without any training, and answer affirmatively with a training-free harness. Our pipeline renders multi-view images of a 3D object, uses a vision-language model to analyze its semantic parts, prompts a generative image model to paint a color-coded part segmentation mask, reprojects it onto the geometry, and fits a superquadric primitive to each part via parameter optimization. The approach contains no learned parameters: it is category-agnostic and orientation-invariant, properties that previous learning-based models struggled with. Its accuracy ceiling rises with future generative-model improvements, which we confirm with a ground-truth segmentation study showing that part segmentation, not primitive fitting, is the current accuracy bottleneck. On HumanPrim and Toys4K, our method achieves the lowest Chamfer distance among all evaluated methods, using 5--9 primitives per object on average.

</details>


### [59] [Hierarchical Classification via Cascading Feature Elimination: Application to Human Phenotype Ontology-Aligned Facial Phenotyping (FaceMesh2HPO)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05585)
*Fabio Hellmann,Alexander Hustinx,Benjamin D. Solomon,GestaltMatcher Database Consortium,Tzung-Chien Hsieh,Peter Krawitz,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceMesh2HPO利用3D面部网格（478个地标点）结合分层PointNet管道和级联分类与特征消除，从2D图像中分类面部表型描述符，并映射到人类表型本体（HPO）。在124位临床医生标注的10种疾病（107个HPO术语）和对照组数据上，最佳模型结合3D网格、面部轮廓和人口统计元数据，AUROC达到0.55-0.89，父节点性能优于叶节点，外部验证泛化性因疾病而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断中需要将面部表型与标准化的本体体系（HPO）关联，以支持综合征患者的精准识别，但现有方法缺乏可解释的、与本体对齐的面部分类框架。

Method: 从2D图像生成478个地标点的3D面部网格，构建分层PointNet管道，采用级联分类和特征消除策略，融合3D网格、面部轮廓和人口统计元数据作为输入特征。

Result: 最佳模型AUROC为0.55-0.89，父节点性能优于叶节点术语；外部验证显示不同疾病的泛化性存在差异；罕见叶节点术语的性能仍然有限。

Conclusion: 对3D面部几何进行分层建模能够实现可解释的、与本体关联的表型分类，但需要改进数据多样性和特征选择策略以增强鲁棒性和临床实用性。

Abstract: FaceMesh2HPO is a framework for classifying facial phenotypic descriptors aligned with the Human Phenotype Ontology (HPO) to support clinical diagnosis. Using annotations from 124 clinicians across 10 disorders (107 HPO terms) combined with non-syndromic controls, we generated 3D facial meshes (478 landmarks) from 2D images and trained a hierarchical PointNet-based pipeline with cascading classification and feature elimination. The best models, incorporating 3D meshes, facial outline, and demographic metadata, achieved AUROCs between ~0.55 and ~0.89, with higher performance at parent nodes than leaf terms. External validation showed variable generalizability across disorders. Results demonstrate that hierarchical modeling of 3D facial geometry enables interpretable, ontology-linked phenotype classification, though performance on rare leaf terms remains limited. Improved data diversity and feature selection strategies are needed to enhance robustness and clinical utility.

</details>


### [60] [Patch Knowledge Transfer for Efficient AI-Generated Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05605)
*Jiquan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出补丁知识迁移（PKT）框架，通过知识蒸馏实现AI生成图像质量评估中视觉表征能力与推理效率的协同优化，在保持与教师模型可比性能的同时降低67.7%的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像质量评估方法存在两难：复杂特征提取方法性能好但计算成本高，无法实时推理；简单图像缩放方法计算高效但评估精度显著不足。亟需一种能兼顾效率与精度的优化方案。

Method: 提出PKT知识蒸馏优化框架，采用双模型架构：教师模型通过局部-全局混合处理提供高质量监督信号，学生模型仅依赖全局处理，通过多层级知识迁移机制继承教师模型的表征能力。

Result: 在4个AIGIQA数据库上的实验表明，PKT框架使学生模型在保持与教师模型可比性能的同时，计算成本降低67.7%，且相比现有方法实现了模型效率与评估精度的更优平衡。

Conclusion: PKT框架通过知识蒸馏有效解决了AI生成图像质量评估中效率与精度的矛盾，为大规模生成图像的实时质量评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of image generation technologies, perceptual quality assessment of AI-generated images has emerged as a crucial research direction in computer vision. The core challenge of this task lies in achieving efficient quality assessment for massive generated images. Current mainstream approaches exhibit two key limitations: 1) Methods employing complex feature extraction strategies, while improving performance, incur prohibitive computational costs that hinder real-time inference; 2) Simple image scaling-based solutions, despite their computational efficiency, demonstrate significantly inferior assessment accuracy. To address this critical issue, we propose Patch Knowledge Transfer (PKT), a knowledge distillation-based optimization framework that achieves synergistic optimization of visual representation capability and inference efficiency through an innovative multi-level knowledge transfer mechanism. Specifically, we design a dual-model architecture: a teacher model with local-global hybrid processing provides high-quality supervision signals, while a student model relying solely on global processing efficiently inherits the teacher's representation capacity through multi-level supervision. Extensive experiments conducted on 4 AIGIQA databases demonstrate that the PKT framework enables the student model to maintain performance comparable to the teacher while reducing computational costs by 67.7\%. Furthermore, compared to existing methods, our approach achieves a superior balance between model efficiency and assessment accuracy.

</details>


### [61] [Cross-Contextual Vision-Language Adaptation with LoRA for Personalized Severe Adverse Event Detection in Clinical Wound Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05625)
*Aditi Naiknaware,Jian Sun,Aminreza Khandan,Shengyang Huang,Sean Dow,Bijan Najafi,Salimeh Sekeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态框架，通过融合临床笔记与伤口视觉描述，结合双流LoRA融合机制和伤口特异性OOD检测，实现自动化伤口监测和严重不良事件(SAE)的早期识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伤口监测是临床中重要但未被充分解决的问题，现有VLM缺乏领域特异性知识，难以整合异质临床信息并检测分布外样本。

Method: 基于冻结的BiomedCLIP骨干，采用双流LoRA框架编码成对临床笔记和伤口视觉描述，通过跨上下文LoRA融合机制实现信息交互；提出伤口特异性OOD检测框架，结合语义匹配、视觉典型性、字幕-文本对齐和字幕-视觉对齐生成统一SAE评分，并引入协变量一致性和时间漂移惩罚以捕捉愈合动态。

Result: 在纵向伤口数据集上的实验表明，该方法在伤口愈合评估和SAE检测方面均取得了有前景的性能。

Conclusion: 语义丰富且时间感知的视觉语言系统在临床伤口监测和早期风险识别方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Wound monitoring is a critical yet underserved clinical challenge, where timely identification of severe adverse events (SAEs) such as infection, tissue deterioration, and delayed healing can significantly impact patient outcomes. While vision-language models (VLMs) show strong multimodal reasoning, they often lack domain-specific grounding to integrate wound imagery with heterogeneous clinical information, and provide limited mechanisms for detecting cases that diverge from the training distribution. We present a multimodal framework for automated wound monitoring and SAE detection. Our approach leverages paired clinical notes and wound descriptions capturing visual characteristics such as appearance, surrounding skin condition, color changes, and signs of inflammation or healing progression, encoded through a dual-stream Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) framework built on a frozen BiomedCLIP backbone. We introduce a cross-contextual LoRA fusion mechanism enabling information exchange between clinical semantics and visual wound descriptors, producing context-aware multimodal representations without full model fine-tuning. To identify personalized SAEs, we propose a wound-specific out-of-distribution (OOD) detection framework combining semantic matching, visual typicality, caption-text alignment, and caption-visual alignment into a unified SAE (OOD) score. To capture healing dynamics, we incorporate covariate consistency and temporal drift penalties that leverage changes in wound characteristics across visits. Experiments on a longitudinal wound dataset collected through clinical visits show promising performance on both wound healing assessment and SAE detection, highlighting the potential of semantically enriched, temporally aware vision-language systems for clinical wound monitoring and early risk identification.

</details>


### [62] [Taxlifier: Leveraging Disease Taxonomy for Enhanced Multi-Label Classification in Chest Radiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05628)
*Mohammad S. Majdi,Jeffrey J. Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于层级关系的多标签分类方法（损失基和逻辑基），用于胸部X光片中多种胸腔疾病的分类。在三个大型数据集（CheXpert、PADCHEST、NIH）上，两种方法相比基线在准确率、AUC和F1分数上分别提升了12%/11%、13%/10%和24%/12%，显著改善了分类性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片中多病理共存且视觉特征重叠，传统自动分类系统难以准确区分多种胸腔疾病，亟需利用疾病间的层级结构信息提升分类性能。

Method: 提出两种层级多标签分类技术：1) 损失基方法（loss-based），将层级关系直接融入优化过程；2) 逻辑基方法（logit-based），基于父类调整各类的预测概率。在CheXpert、PADCHEST、NIH三个大规模数据集上评估。

Result: 逻辑基方法在准确率、AUC、F1上分别提升12%、13%、24%；损失基方法分别提升11%、10%、12%。进行了统计验证以证明鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 集成领域层级知识能有效提升分类性能并增强输出可解释性，层级多标签分类在胸部放射学计算机辅助诊断系统中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient classification of thoracic diseases in chest X-ray (CXR) images is crucial for timely diagnosis and treatment. However, the presence of multiple pathologies with overlapping visual characteristics poses significant challenges for automated classification systems. In this study, we propose two novel hierarchical multi-label classification techniques, namely the loss-based and logit-based methods, to address these challenges by leveraging the hierarchical relationships among different thoracic pathologies. The loss-based technique integrates hierarchical information directly into the optimization process, while the logit-based method adjusts the predicted probabilities of each class based on its parent class in the disease taxonomy. We evaluate the performance of both techniques using three large-scale CXR datasets: CheXpert (224,316 CXRs), PADCHEST (160,000 CXRs), and NIH (112,120 CXRs). The experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in accuracy, AUC, and F1 scores compared to the baseline method across various pathologies. The logit-based and loss-based methods improve accuracy by 12\% and 11\%, AUC by 13\% and 10\%, and F1 scores by 24\% and 12\%, respectively compared to the baseline. These results represent a substantial improvement over the baseline method. Furthermore, we conduct a comprehensive statistical analysis to validate the robustness and reliability of the proposed techniques. The integration of domain-specific hierarchical knowledge not only enhances the classification performance but also provides a more interpretable output for clinical decision support. Our findings highlight the potential of hierarchical multi-label classification in advancing computer-aided diagnosis systems for chest radiography.

</details>


### [63] [Recovering Cloud Microstructures with Cascaded Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05637)
*Hanan Gani,Guy Pulik,Daniel Rosenfeld,Duncan Watson-Parris,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段扩散超分框架，将多光谱云微结构图像分辨率提升4倍，在保持结构保真度的同时增强纹理细节，优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率卫星图像对云微结构观测至关重要，但现有卫星分辨率不足，且传统超分方法难以泛化到卫星光谱图像。

Method: 两阶段扩散超分框架：Stage1利用真实配对数据学习鲁棒退化处理与传感器间对齐；Stage2通过自监督内部降级高分辨率数据优化结构学习与纹理合成。

Result: 在重建精度和视觉质量上均优于SOTA的Transformer和扩散基线模型，能更好捕捉对流塔、云隙等细小云微结构。

Conclusion: 两阶段方法互补：Stage1擅长粗结构保真，Stage2贡献细节与真实感，为改进云微物理分析及AI在气候可持续性应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-resolution satellite imagery is critical for observing fine-scale cloud structures that inform weather modification strategies like cloud seeding for rain-enhancement. However, the spatial resolution of current geostationary and polar-orbiting satellites is often insufficient for capturing small cloud features. Current super-resolution methodologies are suited for natural images and, therefore, struggle to generalize to satellite-captured spectral images of cloud cover. To address this, we propose a two-stage diffusion-based super-resolution framework to enhance the resolution of multi-spectral cloud microstructures by a factor of $4\times$. Specifically, we use inverse diffusion to recover the high resolution properties from low resolution. Stage 1 utilizes real-world paired data to learn robust degradation handling and inter-sensor alignment, while Stage 2 employs a self-supervised internal downgrading of high resolution data to refine structural learning and texture synthesis. Our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art transformer and diffusion-based baselines in both reconstruction accuracy and visual quality. We demonstrate that the two-stage method better captures fine cloud microstructures (e.g. convective turrets and cloud gaps) that are crucial for effective cloud seeding decisions. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of the two stages: Stage 1 excels in coarse structural fidelity, while Stage 2 contributes enhanced detail and realism. These results highlight a practical path toward improving cloud microphysics analysis and as a step towards utilizing AI for climate and sustainability. Our code and models are publicly available at: https://github.com/hananshafi/superresolution-cloud-microphysics.

</details>


### [64] [VEIL: How Visual Encoding Hijacking Induces Bias In Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05641)
*Suranjana Sooraj,Xuyang Chen,Madhumitha Venkatesan,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEIL系统研究了时间序列图表分类中，视觉编码如何影响模型学到的表征，发现图表设计选择会显著影响模型学习结果，而注意力引导训练仅在特定条件下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列图表分类方法日益普及，但模型究竟是在学习底层时间模式还是依赖图表设计引入的视觉线索尚不清楚，亟需系统性研究编码方式对表征学习的影响。

Method: 通过相似性分析、迁移性分析和归因分析三个互补的诊断维度，系统研究图表编码对学习表征的影响，并评估注意力引导训练的效果。

Result: 注意力引导训练仅在跨诊断方法一致识别出编码敏感性的情况下能够缓解其影响，否则提供有限或负面的收益。图表编码设计显著塑造了学到的表征。

Conclusion: 图表时间序列分类应被视为表征与测量问题而非简单的建模决策，视觉化设计选择会影响视觉模型的感知方式，需将图形感知从人类读者扩展至视觉模型。

Abstract: Rendering time series as chart images for CNN-based classification has become increasingly common in time-series classification (TSC). However, it remains unclear whether models learn underlying temporal patterns or rely on encoding-specific visual cues introduced by chart design. We present VEIL: a systematic study examining how chart encodings influence learned representations through complementary analyses of similarity, transferability, and attribution. Attention-guided training appears to mitigate this effect when encoding sensitivity is consistently identified across diagnostics, but provides limited or negative benefit when such signals are absent. These findings position VEIL within the broader question of how machines perceive visualizations -- extending graphical perception from human readers to vision models -- and show that visualization design choices shape learned representations in ways that warrant treating chart-based TSC as a representation and measurement problem rather than a simple modeling decision.

</details>


### [65] [REVIVE: A Multi-Modal Framework for Vandalism Detection and Recovery in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05649)
*Abdullah Tariq Choudhry,Tapadhir Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REVIVE框架，针对自动驾驶车辆摄像头被故意遮挡（VOA）后的图像恢复问题，整合了检测、分割、类型感知修复（BLIP引导的Stable Diffusion、像素替换、中值滤波）和质量门控机制。实验表明，在参考对齐条件下，直接像素替换可将目标检测召回率从0.588恢复到0.967，而质量门控确保了恢复后图像不低于原始受损帧的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆面临日益严重的蓄意遮挡攻击（VOA）威胁，现有检测框架虽能识别被破坏图像，但遮挡后相机流的实用恢复问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: REVIVE框架包含四个步骤：(1) 二元VOA检测；(2) 多类VOA模式识别；(3) EfficientNet-based U-Net分割；(4) 类型感知恢复：BLIP引导的Stable Diffusion修复、直接像素替换或自适应中值滤波。同时引入质量门控机制过滤恢复效果不佳的候选图像。

Result: Stable Diffusion各模式SSIM为0.667-0.867，PSNR为15.4-26.7dB；直接像素替换在参考对齐条件下近乎完美重建。未恢复VOAs下YOLOv8l召回率0.588，直接像素替换恢复至0.967召回率、0.970 F1分数。无质量门控时召回率降至0.304，有质量门控时恢复至0.608。

Conclusion: REVIVE提供了一套从VOA攻击中恢复的结构化框架，通过类型感知修复和质量门控确保恢复后的感知流不低于原始受损帧的性能，有效提升了自动驾驶视觉系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) face increasing threats from vandalism-induced occlusion attacks (VOAs) that compromise camera-based perception. While detection frameworks can identify vandalized images, restoring camera-stream utility after physical occlusion remains underexplored. This paper presents present the Recovery and Enhancement of Vandalized Images for Vision Excellence (REVIVE) framework, a vandalism recovery pipeline integrating: (1) binary VOA detection, (2) multi-class VOA pattern identification, (3) EfficientNet-based U-Net segmentation, and (4) type-aware recovery using Bootstrapping Language-Image Pre-training (BLIP)-guided Stable Diffusion inpainting, direct pixel replacement, or adaptive median filtering. Stable Diffusion shows variable reconstruction performance (per-pattern SSIM 0.667-0.867, PSNR 15.4-26.7dB) across VOA patterns, while aligned direct pixel replacement achieves near-identical reconstruction under the aligned-reference condition. On 500 tracked clean/vandalized image pairs, unrecovered VOAs reduce YOLOv8l object-detection recall to 0.588, while direct pixel replacement restores recall to 0.967 and F1-score to 0.970 under that aligned-reference condition. LaMa, Telea, and Navier-Stokes baselines improve image similarity but provide more limited downstream detection recovery, and Stable Diffusion is treated as an asynchronous recovery branch subject to a quality gate rather than a blocking real-time perception step. We evaluate a reference-available quality gate that filters recovered candidates before downstream use: without it, type-aware routing degrades per-image recall to 0.304, whereas with it, recall returns to 0.608, at or above the unrecovered baseline, ensuring the forwarded stream is never worse than the unrecovered frame. REVIVE therefore, provides a structured recovery framework from VOAs in AVs.

</details>


### [66] [Clustered Codebook Quantization for 2D Gaussian-based Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05667)
*Runze Cheng,Yicheng Zhan,Josef Spjut,Kaan Akşit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cluster-Guided Vector Quantization (CGVQ)方法，通过对高斯参数进行同质分组后再量化，在保持视觉质量的同时将bpp降低20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯图像表示虽能通过紧凑参数保持高保真度，但存储大量浮点参数会降低高保真目标下的率失真效率。

Method: 提出Cluster-Guided Vector Quantization (CGVQ)，先将高斯参数划分为同质组，再进行矢量量化压缩。

Result: 相比基线方法，CGVQ在保持同等视觉质量的同时，bpp降低了20%。

Conclusion: 通过对高斯参数进行聚类引导的矢量量化，可以有效提升高斯表示下的率失真性能。

Abstract: Gaussian-based image representations effectively model image content using compact parametric primitives while preserving high visual fidelity, yet storing a large number of floating-point parameters per primitive degrades rate-distortion efficiency at higher fidelity targets. To improve the rate-distortion performance in Gaussian representation, we present our Cluster-Guided Vector Quantization (CGVQ), a Gaussian primitive based image compression method. Our key idea is to partition Gaussian parameters further into homogeneous groups prior to quantization, enabling higher compression efficiency and accurate parameter reconstruction. In practice, our extensive experiments show that CGVQ decreases the bpp by 20% with respect to our baseline, while maintaining on-par visual quality

</details>


### [67] [Robust Face Super-Resolution and Recognition Through Multi-Feature Aggregation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05702)
*Marcelo dos Santos,Rayson Laroca,João Carlos Raposo Neves,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FASR++，一种基于扩散模型的超分辨率算法，利用多个低质量辅助图像的特征来增强低分辨率监控图像，在提升图像质量的同时最小化身份失真，并在人脸识别任务上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控环境中的图像常受低分辨率、姿态变化、光照不均和遮挡等影响，导致人脸识别算法性能下降。现有超分辨率算法虽能增强图像细节，但容易造成图像畸变和身份信息丢失，因此需要引入额外信息来提升识别鲁棒性。

Method: FASR++基于扩散模型，利用参考低分辨率图像以及从多个辅助低质量图像中提取的特征，生成超分辨率输出，无需显式提供软属性或计算函数梯度来引导重建过程，从而最小化身份失真。

Result: 在两个标准人脸识别数据集上，FASR++在验证、人脸识别任务以及PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS等图像质量指标上均达到了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: FASR++作为预处理步骤，能够生成高质量图像，显著提升人脸识别任务的性能，有效解决了监控场景下低质量图像的人脸识别难题。

Abstract: Images acquired in surveillance environments often suffer from conditions such as low resolution, variations in pose, irregular illumination, and occlusions. Due to the low quality of these images, face recognition algorithms often struggle. This major limitation can be addressed by employing super-resolution techniques that enhance the details of the image. However, due to the high degree of difficulty of the problem, most super-resolution algorithms tend to cause distortions in the image and in the individual's identity. Thus, additional information must be incorporated into the processing to improve recognition robustness. In this regard, surveillance cameras can capture multiple images, even at low quality, and the data extracted from these images, such as consecutive video frames, can significantly enhance both super-resolution and facial recognition. In this work, we introduce FASR++, a diffusion-model-based super-resolution algorithm. It leverages a reference low-resolution image and features extracted from multiple auxiliary low-quality images to generate a super-resolved output, minimizing distortions in the individual's identity. Our approach recovers facial features without explicitly providing soft attributes or computing a function gradient to guide the reconstruction process. FASR++ generates high-quality images that can considerably improve performance in face recognition tasks when used as a pre-processing step. We validate our approach on two standard face recognition datasets and attain state-of-the-art results for verification, face recognition, and image quality metrics such as PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS.

</details>


### [68] [Scene Graph Thinking: Reinforcing Structured Visual Reasoning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05716)
*Zhiwei Yang,Yuanchen Wu,Nan Zhang,Yucong Meng,Ke Yan,Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene Graph Thinking (SaGe)范式，通过引入场景图结构化表示增强多模态大语言模型的细粒度视觉推理能力。该方法构建了自动化数据引擎将平面图文数据转为结构化场景图，并基于此生成120K高质量训练数据；同时提出两阶段图对齐后训练范式（监督微调+强化微调），利用节点代理图奖励优化图探索。在8个多模态基准上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs聚焦于孤立目标而忽视结构化关系，导致在视觉密集型任务中目标导航效率低下，缺乏细粒度结构化推理能力。

Method: (1) 自动化数据引擎：将平面图像-文本语料转换为结构化场景图，以层次化实体为节点、多样化视觉关系为边；(2) 构建120K高质量训练数据，从场景图中采样推理轨迹；(3) 两阶段图对齐后训练范式：监督微调使MLLMs内化结构化推理，强化微调引入节点代理图奖励以巩固高效图探索。

Result: 在8个多模态基准测试上取得显著性能提升，在细粒度感知和推理任务上展现出强有效性。

Conclusion: 显式场景图表示结合图对齐训练能有效增强MLLMs的结构化视觉推理能力，尤其适用于需要细粒度感知的视觉密集型任务。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong perception and reasoning capabilities. However, most existing models focus on isolated objects and neglect structured relationships for efficient target navigation, limiting their performance on visually intensive tasks. To address this challenge, we introduce Scene Graph Thinking (SaGe), a novel paradigm that enables fine-grained and structured visual reasoning through explicit scene-graph representations. Specifically, we first introduce an automated data engine that converts flat image-text corpora into structured scene graphs, where hierarchical entities constitute the nodes and diverse visual relations define the edges. Building upon this, we construct 120K high-quality training data by sampling reasoning traces from scene graphs. Then, two-stage graph-aligned post-training paradigms are introduced, where supervised fine-tuning internalizes MLLMs with structured reasoning, and subsequent reinforcement fine-tuning proposes node-as-proxy graph rewards to consolidate efficient graph exploration. With curated data and graph-aligned training, our approach achieves significant improvements across eight multimodal benchmarks, demonstrating strong effectiveness on fine-grained perception and reasoning tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/zwyang6/SaGe.

</details>


### [69] [Association Restoration Test: Revealing Restorable Shortcuts after Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05726)
*Amy Lu,Changxiu Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出关联恢复测试（ART），一种用于诊断分类器中标签-属性捷径关联是否被真正遗忘的后验方法，通过在多个数据集上实验证明输出指标、表征探针和ART衡量了捷径缓解的不同方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（输出鲁棒性和表征探针）无法判断被保留的关联是否仍能被原始分类器功能性地使用，需要一种能检测功能性捷径可恢复性的诊断方法。

Method: 提出关联恢复测试（ART），通过估计类别条件关联方向、放大残差成分，再用原始分类器头部评估修改后的特征，从而诊断功能性捷径的可恢复性。

Result: 在Waterbirds、CelebA、SpuCoDogs和ISIC时间戳伪影扩展数据集上，实验表明输出指标、表征探针和ART分别刻画了捷径缓解的不同方面。

Conclusion: 呼吁对去学习和捷径缓解方法采用恢复感知评估，这些方法应针对学习到的关联而非单个类别或概念。

Abstract: Association unlearning aims to disable learned label-attribute shortcuts while preserving task performance. Existing evaluations mainly measure output-level robustness or probe whether shortcut attributes remain readable in frozen features, but neither test determines whether a retained association remains functionally usable by the original classifier. We propose the Association Restoration Test (ART), a post-hoc diagnostic for functional shortcut restorability. ART estimates class-conditional association directions, amplifies residual components, and evaluates the modified features with the original classifier head. Across Waterbirds, CelebA, SpuCoDogs, and an ISIC timestamp-artifact extension, we show that output metrics, representation probes, and ART characterize distinct aspects of shortcut mitigation. These findings motivate restoration-aware evaluation for unlearning and shortcut-mitigation methods that target learned associations rather than individual classes or concepts.

</details>


### [70] [SAMPLe: SAM-based Optimizer for Prompt Learning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05727)
*Hossein Rajoli,Fatemeh Lotfi,Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Hossein Kashiani,Xiaolong Ma,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMPLe是一个即插即用的锐度感知优化器，通过考虑损失景观锐度来增强提示泛化能力，解决视觉语言模型提示学习中性能与泛化之间的困境，在多种提示学习框架上一致优于现有优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(如CLIP)的提示学习面临性能-泛化困境：提示调优能在已知分布上达到高精度，但往往损害了对未见数据的泛化能力。有限的提示参数容易过拟合训练数据，导致任务特定性能与保持泛化能力之间的权衡。

Method: 提出SAMPLe (Sharpness-Aware Minimization Prompt Learning)，一种锐度感知优化器。通过考虑损失景观的锐度来增强提示泛化性，在每个优化步骤中满足目标函数约束条件，基于局部曲率和梯度属性动态适应当前优化状态，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 将SAMPLe集成到CoOp、CoCoOp、MaPLe、TCP和Co-Prompt等多种提示学习框架中，实验表明SAMPLe提升了这些框架的性能，并在多种设置下一致优于现有优化器。

Conclusion: SAMPLe作为一种模型无关的提示学习解决方案，有效缓解了过拟合并提升了对未见数据的适应性，为提示学习中的泛化问题提供了鲁棒的优化方法。

Abstract: Pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) like CLIP have proven highly effective as foundation models for various downstream applications. However, prompt learning in VLMs encounters a performance-generalization dilemma: while prompts can be tuned to achieve high accuracy on seen distributions, this tuning process often undermines their generalizability to unseen data. The limited set of learnable prompts, which contextualize and condition the input to steer it toward the task within the pretrained VLM, tends to overfit the training data, leading to a trade-off between task-specific performance and preserving generalization. To address this dilemma, we introduce SAMPLe (Sharpness-Aware Minimization Prompt Learning), a plug-in sharpness-aware optimizer that enhances prompt generalizability by accounting for loss landscape sharpness. Unlike conventional methods, SAMPLe balances exploration and exploitation by satisfying objective function constraints at each step, dynamically adapting to the current optimization state based on the local curvature and gradient properties. This approach reduces overfitting on seen distributions and improves adaptability to unseen data, preserving the generalization potential of pre-trained VLM models. We integrate SAMPLe into multiple prompt learning frameworks, including CoOp, CoCoOp, MaPLe, TCP, and Co-Prompt, demonstrating its effectiveness across diverse methods. Experiments show that SAMPLe elevates prompt learning frameworks and consistently outperforms existing optimizers across diverse settings, establishing itself as a robust, model-agnostic solution for prompt learning.

</details>


### [71] [ARMS: Anchor-Relational Motion Streaming for Seamless Solo-Social Motion Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05733)
*Huakun Liu,Qing Yu,Kent Fujiwara,Hideaki Uchiyama,Kiyoshi Kiyokawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMS提出了一个锚定关系运动流框架，通过动力学非对称表示和因果关系扩散模型，实现单人动作与人际交互的无缝切换和长时序运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法生成固定长度运动片段且假设静态代理配置，无法处理单人-社交转换，也不适用于长时增量生成。

Method: 提出ARMS框架：1) 动力学非对称表示，解耦个体时间演化与通过伙伴参考相对平移项实现的人际对齐；2) 因果潜在空间上的因果关系扩散模型，逐段渐进细化运动；3) 模式感知关系门控，激活或屏蔽跨代理连接以支持单人与交互生成。

Result: 相比以交互为中心的基线方法，ARMS在转换平滑性和社交连贯性上取得改进，同时在人际交互基准上达到竞争性结果。

Conclusion: ARMS通过统一的因果生成过程有效支持单人运动和人际交互之间的无缝切换，兼顾长时稳定性和空间一致性。

Abstract: Generating temporally continuous and socially coherent human motion from text remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in realistic streams where people act alone, enter interactions, and later disengage. Most existing methods generate fixed-length motion clips under static agent configurations, which makes them brittle to solo-social transitions and unsuitable for incremental generation over long horizons. We propose ARMS, an Anchor-Relational Motion Streaming framework that unifies solo motion and human-human interaction within a single causal generative process. ARMS introduces a dynamics-asymmetric representation that decouples per-person temporal evolution from inter-person alignment via a partner-referenced relative-translation term, enabling seamless switching of social coupling without sacrificing long-horizon stability or spatial consistency between agents. On top of a causal latent space, a causal relational diffusion model progressively refines motion segment by segment using only past context, capturing both intra-person temporal dependencies and inter-person relations. Mode-aware relational gating activates or masks cross-agent connections, allowing the same model to support both solo and interaction generation.
